# huichuandenvrenhenshaoxuanyundongxie，jinnianliuxing：malizhenxie+qunzi，shihexiaogezi
fuguchaodelaixiyijibuduanliuxing，rangyiqiegenfuguganzhanbiandedanpinduzhongxinjinrudaodajiadeshixian，bingqiebeishishangdarenmenzhengxiangzhuipeng。nidiyigexiangdaodefuguxidanpinshishenmeni？fuguganmanfende“malizhenxie”yidingshibangshangyoumingde！malizhenxiezenmechuan？dangranpeichangqungengdaigan！
jinnianxiatian，xiangxinhenduoxiaojiejieyouyaobapiaoyixianqidenvshenxichangqunnachulaile，naihehuanmeizhaodaoyishuangheshidedanxiekeyijinxingdapei。huanzaichuanyundongxiedeniganjintaotaidiao，piaoyidechangqun+fugudemalizhenxiecaishijuepei，shuichuanshuihaokandejiezou！shangshenxianshouxiaoguoyijue，tuichangyimibadejishigan，rangnicongcimishangzhetaochuanda，yuechuanyueyouyangqigan！
tiwenyi、malizhenxiedetedianheyoushizainali？
✔waixingfugu，zidaiyouyaqixi。
✔xiexinglitijingzhi，xieshenkongjianda，shihejiaohou、jiaopangderenqun。
✔duoweicugensheji，tuxianshengaoyoujianshaojiaobuyali。
tiwener、malizhenxieshiheshenmerenqun？
renqun1、jiaoxiaodegezi
xiaogezichuanpingdixiehuixiandeguoyujiaoxiao，chuanxigaogendexieziyouganjuezhongliangzhichengbuqilai。malizhenxieduobanshicugendesheji，keyirangjiaobuxiandegengyouwenguxing，bingqiekeyichongdangxigaogenxiedezenggaoxianshouzuoyong，rangxiaogezirenqunbagaoqilai。
renqun2、chuangaogenxiedekunnanhu
gaogenxiebingfeisuoyourendunengjiayu，youxiebuhuichuangaogenxiedenvsheng，jiuyaochuanxiangmalizhenxiezhezhongduobandaiyoucugenshejidexiezi，jinengmanzuchuangaogenxiedexuqiu，youbuhuixingzoukunnan，jiyashuangjiao。
renqun3、jiaoxingpianda、pangderen
pingjiemalizhenxiebijiaolitidebanxing，buhuihenqingbo，suoyihenshiheyixiejiaoxingpianpanghejiaozhipiankuanderenqunchuan，buxiangputongdedanxiehuozhegaogenxieyiyang，rongyijiyazhaojiaozhi，suibianzouliangbujiurongyijiyashoushang。
★butongdejiaoxingshihetiaoxuandemalizhenxiekeyishi：
jiaobeihoudenvshengkeyixuanzebijiaoqiankoushejidemalizhenxie，geijiaobeichongzudehuodongkongjian；jiaozhikuanderenkeyixuanzeyuantouhuozhefangtoushejidemalizhenxie，rangjianjudadejiaozhiyenengziyoushenzhan，bushouyueshu。
zhangwochangqun+malizhenxiedeyangqidapeifaze
# fazeyi：sexidapeifaze
（1）xiepeihemouyijiandanpintongse
jiangjiu“zhengqigan”，keyicankaoshenshangchuandefuzhuangdeyanse，laixuanzexiangtongyansedemalizhenxiejinxingdapei。ruguochangqunshibanqundekuanshi，xiezikeyiheshangyitongse；ruguochangqunshilianyiqun，najiukeyihequnshenyanseyiti。
→zhuyi：shangyi+banqunmoshishi，xiezideyansezuihaoheshangyitongse，zhongjianxianggezhaobutongyansedebanqun，keyixingchengshijueshangdeyizhongfengegan，cengcifenming。ruguoshibanqunhexiezitongse，zehuixiandexiabanshentongsemianjiguoda，bijiaorongyixianai。
（2）xieyuqunshenhuasexiangjin
zheshiyizhongxijiehuyingdefangfa，dangbuzhidaozenmexuanzexiezideyansegenqunzipeiduideshihou，keyiguanchaqunzishenshangdehuase，youxiequnzidaiyouyinhua，leisisuihuaqundekuanshi，huiyouyigezhusediao，genzhaozhegezhusediaolaitiaoxuanxiangtonghuoxiangjindeyanselaizuoweimalizhenxiedeyanse，henqiangyan。
（3）shen/qianse+liangse=youxiaoxijing
danghenjichuhuozhehenqiandandeyanse，dapeizhaohenxianliangdeyansedexiezideshihou，jiuhuixingchengyizhongshijueshangdeqiangliefancha，xiyinrenmuguangdejilvjiuhuidadazengjia。
shenchendeyansedapeiliangsexiandegewaiyoufugugan；qiansedequnzidapeiliangsedexiezizerangzhengtiduxiandehenqingliang，jijuyouxiaridefenweigan。

changqunhexiezidemianliaorouruanduhuozheyingtingdudubijiaoxiediao，chuanshangshennengdazaobijiaotongyidefengge。

# huodeqianaliCEOtouzidexijiezhachuanzhaodaoleliuliangmima，zhachuansaidaoweiherucihuore？

### **shichangguimoyichao2000yi，zengsupaimingdiyi**

**xiayouzeshixingxingsesedeshaokaomendian，bujufensan。**zhaimencanyanshujuxianshi，muqianshaokaoxingyeguimobijiaodadepinpaiyoujiutianjiaheiniukaorouliaoli，mendianshuliangwei1105jia，xijiezhachuan1115jia，jinsuiqihoudekuafuzhachuan702jia，fengkuangkaochi618jiamendian，chuanyishizushaokaoyou603jia，qiyudabufenpinpaiguimodadubudao500jia，yiliangbaijiajuduo。

**02****xijie、kuafuzhachuanjingyingdeguanjianyaosuyounaxie？

### **“xiaoermei”chengweixiaochijiangbenzengxiaodeyunyingmima**

**ciwai，zaichudancaozuoshangshixianlebiaozhunhua，jiangyidankongzhizai90miao。**biruxijiedemeijiadiandupeiyoujiqichuanchuanshebei，jidaditigaolechuanchuanxiaolv。zhachuandeshijian、shuandiaoliaoduyoutongyibiaozhun，huanyanfalejiugonggezhalu，bingyangeguidingmeiyikuandanpindezhazhishijian，baozhenglechucanxiaolv。zai10pingmidechufangdongxianshejizhong，congguodegaodudaocaozuotaideweizhibaifangdujingguodiaozheng，jinliangrangyuangongpingyicaozuo，jiangdipilaodu，tigaoxiaolv。
**kuafudechuangshirenyuanzeluyejianchi“xiaomendian，daliansuo，quangongyinglian”delinian。**tafansizhiqiandexishaoyeroujiamomoshi，renweixiangbidadianmoxing，30pingyixiadedian，wulunlirunhuanshikekongxing，dugengjuyoushi。
**suoyikuafuzhachuanyezhuiqiukuaihexiaolv。**xuanpinshang，teyixuanzele“chuanxiao，rongyizuozoushi”deleshanzhachuan。yongduchuangyaocaoluyou，rangzhachuanchanshengfuhekougan，qiguanwangxianshi，kuafutouru10jiwanyuanyanfayou，zhanlekaiyeqianzijintourude10%zuoyou。juetouziyehuodelehuibao，dutedekouweishiqifugoulvgaoda50%-60%。

### **wanshandegongyingliantixi，shishixianwandiankuozhangdebaozhang**
nengshixianrucigaodechupinxiaolv，biranlibukaiwanshandegongyingliantixi。**xijiedechuangshirenwangkuankuancengzongjiechubaichengwandiandepinleijiyin：jiegoufuza、chukoujiandan。**jiegoufuza，shizhihoutailiantiaosuifuzadannengbeigongyinglianquantaoyouhua，huozhenengzuodaogongyinglianquanpei。neibujiegoufuzadebufenyougongsilaiwancheng，dabaofengzhuanghaohoutongyishuchugeimendian，mendianwanquanwuxucaoxinchanpinzhizuo，jiangdifuzadu，tishengxiaolv。chukoujiandan ，shizhiqianduanmendiancaozuojiandan，buxutaifansuodeliuchengyujingyingnengli。chanpinjiandanyicaozuo，congyunyingshangjiejue20~30%dexiaolvwenti。

# 【fuwu】tuishuitisuweichukouqiyefazhantiandongneng
weitishengchukouqiyeyingduibuduanbianhuaguojihuanjingde“yingshili”，huanjiechukouqiyezijinyali，2022nian“woweinashuirenjiaofeirenbanshishijibianminbanshuichunfengxingdong”yilai，guojiashuiwuzongjuyangzhoushihanjiangqushuiwujushishichukouqiye“yiqiyice”,dongtaijiancechukouqiyechukoutuishuijinebianhua，zhudongtixingqiyeshenbaotuishui，tongshikaipituishui“lvse”tongdao，jianhuabaosongliucheng、jiakuaineibushenhe，rangbaxiangfuwugengjingzhun。

shouhaiwaiyiqingyingxiang，akemujiedaoledaliangdongnanyahuiliudingdan，jinnianshangbannianshixianchukouxiaoshou3.3yiyuan，tongbizengchang97.5%，shangbannianleijibanlichukoutuishui2907wanyuan，tongbizengchang51.9%。tuishuikuaisudaozhang，weiqiyejishitigonglezhouzhuanzijin，rangqiyezaimianduiyuancailiaojiageshangzhang、wuliushouzu、gongyingshanggonghuobujishi、channenggenbushangdengkunjingshi，duyiyipingwenguodu。chongzudexianjinliu，yerangxujiuquanxintaiyufaleguan：“qianjigeyuehuiliudingdanshizaitaiduo，womenbudebuweituoleqitagongchangdaigong，zuijinwomenzijidechannengyijingnengyingfule。womenhuangenjindiaoyanlejinnianqiujioumeidiquhuwaiyongpindeliuxingkuanshijiyongliaoxuqiu，zhidingxiayibudeshengchanjihuayiyingxiaofangan，zhengquzhuazhusijidu‘huangjinqi’zaichuangjiaji。”
yangzhouyangjiedianzikejigufenyouxiangongsi（jiancheng“yangjiekeji”）shiyijiacongshibandaotifenliqijianxinpianshejizhizao、qijianfengzhuangceshi、zhongduanxiaoshouyufuwudeshangshigongsi，jinliangnianchukoutuishuijunzai1yiyuanzuoyou。jinjinnianqian7geyue，yangjiekejijiubanlile9586wanyuanchukoutuishui。qiyecaiwuduwenwenshuo：“shoumeiyuanduirenminbihuilvchixuxiajiangyingxiang，womenlianyuelaihuiduisunshijiaoda，erwomenweilexiaoxinhaojichengceshifengzhuangkuochan，touruleshuyiyuanzijinjianchangfang、goushebei，zijinyalihenda。chukoutuishuidejishidaozhang，panhuolewomendezijinliu，baozhanglewomenshengchanyanfadeyouxujinxing。”

**“dadoufuhanxiaoxuewen”chusairuxuanshipin**

# yaodiandadanshiruhelianchengde？jiaokeshushilianhejianyaoanli
zhetian，yimingxiaohuozijindiangoumaizhiliaodoudoudechanpin。dianyuanyinhuajingguozhengquedelianhejianyao，zuihoudachengle“weiAsuanrugao+luhuiningjiao+fufangzhenzhuanchuangpian+kongyoujimianzheli+nanshiruye”dexiaoshou，chengjiaojinejin200yuan。
yiqilaikankanzhecijiaokeshushidelianhejianyaoanliba。
zhetian，yimingxiaohuozijindiangoumaizhiliaodoudoudechanpin。
yinhuaguanchagukezhihoupanduanshixunchangxingcuochuang，yushidailinggukedaolewaiyongyaozhuanqu。
**yinhuashouxiannaleweiAsuanrugao，bingqiexianggukejianyidao：**“zhegeshizhuanmenzhiliaocuochuangdeyaogao，meitianwanshangshuiqianyongyicijiukeyile。”
zheweixiaohuoyaoleyaoyaogao：“zhegechanpinxiaoguozenmeyang？”
**yinhuashuo：**“zheshiyigechangyongyao，nikeyikanyixiashuomingshu，bingqiebayaogaozhanshigeigukekan。”
xiaohuokanleyixiayaogao，ranhoushuo：“en，haoba，jiunazhegeba！”
**yinhuajieguoyaogaogengukejinyibujieshidao：**“nizaishiyongyaogaodeshihou，pingshiyinshiyaohenzhuyi，ladedongxi，youzhadedongxi，bunengchio，yinweihuijiazhongnidecuochuangde。”
xiaohuoshuo：“en，haode，zhidaole，xiexieni。”
**yinhuajixushuo：**“ninshiyongdezhegeweiAsuanrugao，wanshangshuijueqianyong，wojianyinindapeiyizhiluhuiningjiao，keyizaibaitianshiyong”,bingqiebagukeyindaodaoluhuiningjiaosuozaidehuojiaqu。
“luhuiningjiaoyouzhenjingxiaoyan、danhuaseyindezuoyong，doudou、shaishang、wenchongdingyaoyijiqingweitangshangdengdukeyiyong。” yinhuajiezhaoshuo。
“peiheyongshiluhuiningjiao，takeyishinindoudouhuifudegengkuai，erqiekeyibangzhunindanhuayinhen，jianyininmaiyizhishiyixia。”
“youqingweicuochuangshi，zhijieyongluhuijiaoyeshikeyide，jiubuyongshiyongyaowuxingdeyaogao。” yinhuashuodao。
xiaohuozixiangleyixiashuo：“haode，najiuyiqidaishangba！”zhengzhunbeiqujiezhang。
**yinhuajinyibuyindaogukeshuo：**“nizaiyongzhegeyaogaodeshihou，doudouhuimanmandehaoqilai，buguojianyihuanshiyaofuyongyixiezhongchengyao ，rufufangzhenzhuanchuangpian。”
xiaohuozishuo：“weishenmeni？”
**yinhuahuidao：**“koufudefufangzhenzhuanchuangpian，limianyoujinyinhua、pugongying、mutongdengzhongyao，juyouqingrejiedu、liangxiexiaobandengzuoyong，bangnincongneizaijinxingdiaoli，erqiekoufujiawaiyongliangzhepeihe，yekeyibangzhuninhuifudegengkuai。”
xiaohuozishuo：“huibuhuiyongdetaiduole？youmeiyouzhegebiyao？”
**yinhuaxiaozhaoshuo：**“tamenshicongbutongdejiaodulaibangzhuningaishande。zhishiwaiyongdeyaogao，meiyouneizaidediaoli，shiwufajiejuegenbenwentide，doudouhuanhuizaichangchulai。”
xiaohuoshuo：“o，yuanlaishizheyang，nahaode，jiudaishangba！”
**yinhuatongshijianyizheweiguke：**pingshiyinggaiyaoshiyongyixieqingjidechanpin，birushuokongyoudejimianrugaojiashangruye，zheyangpifujiuhuibaochibijiaohaodezhuangtai，bimianchuxiancuochuang。
xiaohuowen：“nanimendianliyoumeiyouzhexiechanpinmai？”
yinhuajinyibuyindaodaogerenhuliyongpinzhuanqu，houlaizheweixiaohuoziyounaleyizhikongyoujimianzhelijiashangnanshiruye。
bizhefenxi，zaizhebichenggongdejiaoyizhong，zuiguanjiandeyouzheyangjidian：
***1、*****bujiyujieshuxiaoshou**
youyixieyaodianrenzaiyindaogukedeshihou，danggukenaleyizhiyaogaohuoyizhongchanpin，jiujizhaoranggukemaidan，gukewangwangzijiyehuiyouzhezhongxiangfa，danshiwomenzaiyindaogukeguochengdangzhongyaodongdekongzhijiezou。

# kesankaoshiyiguadiandajiexi，beikaodexueyuanyidingyaozhuyi！jianyishoucang！

**shangchezhunbei**1、meiyouraocheyizhoujiuzhijiekaimenshangche，yiweizheyixiangzhishizougeguochang，keyoukewu；weizaicheliangqianhoutingliu，ganyingqiwufanying。
zhengquezuofa：shangcheqianxianannishizhenfangxiangraocheyiquan，zairaocheyizhoudeguochengzhongxueyuanyaojiangcheshenanniuanxia，jianchacheliangyouwuyichanghezhouweidaolujiaotongqingkuang。
2、shangcheweiwanquanguanbichemen，diaozhengzuowei、houshijing，weixianquandai。
zhengquezuofa：jiangzuoyihehoushijingdiaozhengdaozuishihezijideweizhijianchasigechemenshifouwanquanguanbi，yidingzuohaozhunbeizaixianquandai，zhiyaoxianquandaiyuyinjiuhuitishinikaishikaoshi，jiumeiyoudiaozhengdejihuile。

zhengquezuofa：shangchehoudengguangkaiguanyaoxianguiwei，quebaosuoyoudengguangchuyuchushizhuangtai，zaiqucaozuo。ruguoshangyiweikaoshengguazaimonidengguangshang，meiyouguanbisuoyoudengguang，niyoumeixixinjianchadaozhiguake，najiufeichangkexile。
**qibucaozuo**1、zhunbeiqibushimeiyouguanchahoufangshifouyoulaiche，congmangqibu，wangjisongshousha，lihefangtaikuai，daozhixihuo。

**xingshi**1、guanchabuzu，qibu、biangengchedao、kaobiantingche、chaocheshihushi“huitouguancha”，huozheguanchaledanbaitoufudubugou，
zhengquezuofa：zaiqibu、tingche、biandao、chaochezhilingxiadahou，niuzhuanjianbanghetoubuyaochaoguo90°，rangkaoguankanjian。
2、ditoukandang，henduoshilihemeiyoucaidaodi。suiranzhichayidiandian，danhuandangjiubushunchang，daozhiduociguadangburu、ditoukandang。
zhengquezuofa：zailianchedeshihoujiuzhuyikefu，duojialianxi，dengzhenzhengzhangwozhihoujiubuhuiyouzhegehuaixiguanle。

# pinyinnandian③：sanpinyinjiepindufangfa
**sanpinyinjiepindufangfa**
zaihanyupinyinli，youyigejieyushengmuheyunmuzhijiandeyin，jiaojieyin。pindushi，youshengmulianjiezuoweijieyindeyunmu“ i 、u 、ü”，zailianqitayunmu。yikouqikuaisudipinduchengyigeyinjie，zhejiushisanpinyinjie。

**1. sanpinliandufa：****liru**m—i—ǎo→miǎomiao；ch—u—ān chuan。
**2.shengjiehemupindufa：****liru**m—i→mi，mi—ǎo→miǎomiao；ch—u→chu，chu—ān chuan。
**3.jieyunhemupindufa：****liru**i—ǎo→iǎo，m—iǎo→miǎomiao；u—ān→uān，ch—uānchuan。
womenduoshucaiyong**sanpinliandufa**。pinduyaolingshishengmuyaoduqing，jieyinyaokuaidu，yunmuyaoduxiang，sanyinlianduhenshundang。laishou“sanpinyin”koujuelaijiashenyinxiangba！

# beishengfulian、jinrinvbao、fengwang、lvxingzazhiqianglietuijiandeqinziyoushengdi——hongjianggushangcheng
NO.13482
▼
**mamaqunawan**-genzhaokebenyouhunan-
SUMMERxiaoshihou，duihuaihuadechuyinxiang，laiyuanyuyuwenkebenshangwangchanglingdenaju“luoyangqinyouruxiangwen，yipianbingxinzaiyuhu”；changdahou，zedui“qingshanyidaotongyunyu，mingyuehecengshiliangxiang”youyoulexindeganwu。
ruoshiwangchanglingzhongyou“longbiao”huaihua，rujinyouhuixiechuzenyangdeshiju？

# kepu| yunmazuotangshaizhiqianbiezuozhejijianshi，burannijiubaizuola！

01zaijianchadeqianjitianbuchitianshi
tangshaizhuyaoshijianchayunfudexietang，shifouyourenshentangniaobingdekeneng，henduoyunfuweilenengrangjianchashunli、yicixingtongguo，zaijianchadeqianjitianjiubuchitianshile。qishiwomenbujianyiyunfuzheyangzuo，yinweitangshaijianchadeshiyunfuzaipingshidezhengchangyinshizhong，tineixietanghanliangdeqingkuang。

02jianchaqianyiwanbuchibuhe

# 【yishanyuan • meirijiankang】zhongliufanghualiaoqijiansandihuanzhedezhongyishiliao
**tianjinyishanyuan**dingweiyuwenhuayishugaoduanyiyangzhongxin，fuwuyujingjinjizhoubiangaoduanlaonianqunti，tongguoweichangzhetigongjuyouanxingan、guishugan、xingfugan、zunyangan、jiazhigandezhongjirenwenguanhuaifuwu，yiquanyuandeyongxinfuwuweichangzhekaiqiquanxindeshenghuofangshi，zhiliyuchengweijingjindiqudegaoduanyanglaodianfan。
2006nianshijieweishengzuzhijiangaizhengdingweiwei“kekongmanxingjibing”，aizhengyijingbuzaishichuantongyiyishangdejuezheng。woguozhiminglinchuangzhongliuxuejia****sunyanyuanshitichu****“****zhongliuzhiliaoyaozuohaochijiuzhandezhunbei****，shadiyiqianzisunbabai，nashijiushidai。xinshidaixia，****womenyaoranggengjingzhundezhiliaobazhongliudapaxiadetongshi，beiwushangdehaoxibaonengxunsuxiufu****”。
zaikangzhongliuzhiliaozhong，****hongxibao、baixibao、xiexiaoban****debianhuashiyishenghehuanzheduhenguanxindewenti，tamendebianhuayuzhiliaoshifoushunlixixixiangguan，****xiexiangdehuifuhenzhongyao****。

shiguangfeishi，buzhibujuejian 2022 nianyeyijingguoqudaban，zongjianshefeichangrongxingdibeipingwei“nianduyouxiuyuangong”，zhefenrongyuduizongjiansheeryanshifenzhengui，tajishigongsilingdaoduitagongzuonenglidekending，yeshijinhougongzuodeyizhongdongliheguli。weici，zongjianshefeichangganxiechejiantongshimendezhichihebangzhu，ganxiegongsilingdaomendeguanxinhezhidao，shizijiqudeleyixiechengji。

# “yi”xiansudi tongshijiansaipaodehesuanbiaobenzhuanyunyuan
10yue24rishangwu7dian，zaiguangyuanshichengqunanheguodongchenghesuancaiyangdian，29suidedegewangbaozhongquanfuwuzhuangzuozaijiashishilijingjingdengdai。“38、39、40……”zixiqingdianwanhesuancaiyangbiaobenshuliang，bingzaizhuanyundanshangqianxia“45guan”hou，taxunsuqichengzhunbeiyunzaibiaobendaoshangxijiaotongwuliuyuanhesuanjizhongzhuanyundian。jinduanshijian，sichuanguangyunjituangufenyouxiangongsichuzuqichefengongsijiashiyuanwangbaozhongyouduoleyigexinshenfen——hesuanbiaoben“zhuanyunyuan”。congguodongchengdaojiaotongwuliugangyue6gonglilucheng，pingchangkaichedanbianyueshiersanfenzhong，wangbaozhongzaizhuanyunhesuanshihuibashijiankongzhizai10fenzhongzuoyou。“womenzaosong1fenzhong，jiancejieguojiugengkuaiyidian。”
yue30fenzhonghou，wangbaozhongcongshangxifanhuinanhecaiyangdian。gezhaomianping，eqiandexiaohanlitebiemingxian。“leibulei？”jizheyouxiexintengdixunwen。“bulei，yidiandubulei。zhishigangchuanfanghufu，youdaiN95kouzhao，youzhaomianping，youxiebuxiguan。”wangbaozhongshuo，zuichuyouxiebenshoubenjiao，dafangxiangpan，caiyoumen、shachedubutaixiguan。weilerangdongzuogengxianshu，youshizaijialitahuanhuishoujiaobingyonggekongyanlian，xunzhaozuiheshidedongzuofudu。“heyixianhesuancaiyangrenyuanxiangbi，womenkaichedexingfuduole。”meitian6dianzhundiancaiyang，wangbaozhonglingchen4：30jiuqichuang。jiandanxishuhou，jiukaishichizaocan。“yibanbuheniunai、xifan，zaofanyiganliangweizhu。” wangbaozhonggaosujizhe，heyixianyihurenyuanyiyang，tayishenfanghufuyaochuanyizhengtian，weilebushangweishengjian，zhinengjinliangshaochihuobuchi。
shizhenzhixiangshangwu8shidian，youyitangzhuanyunkaishi。ruoshipingchang，zhengzhizaogaofeng，zhegedianyeshizuinengjiedandeshihou，1xiaoshidayuenengpaojin10danshengyi。yiqingfashenghou，lizhouquduodijinxinghesuancaiyang，meitianxuyaochuzuchejinjizhuanyunbiaoben，wangbaozhongdiyigexiangguangyunjituanbaomingjiaruhesuanbiaobenzhuanyunduiwu。8yue30riwanshang9dian，tazhuanyunbiaobencongjiangehuidaoguangyuan；9yue1ri，tazaixuefeng821xiaoqucaiyangdianzhuanyun13tang；10yue23ri，tacongnanhezhishangxizhuanyunluchengzuichang……nageshijiandianrenduo？nacizhuanyunshougongzuiwan？natianzhuanyuntangcizuiduo？mianduijizhedecaifang，wangbaozhongyikouqizuochuzongjie。
30fenzhonghou，wangbaozhongcongshangxizhefandaonanhe，30guanhesuancaiyangbiaobenzhengzaizhuangxiang。“30guanyexing，xianpaoyitangzaishuo，miandedengqizhaoji。”shangwu9dianhou，caiyangdeshiminyuelaiyueshao。weiletigaozhuanyunxiaolv，rangcaiyanghesuanjinkuaijinrujianyanhuanjie，wangbaozhongzhihaokaojiamitangcilaiyingqujianyanshijian。congcaiyangyuanshouzhongjieguobiaobenzhuanyunxiang，xiaoxinyiyidifangzaicheshang，wangbaozhongqidongyinqing，youyitangzhuanyunkaishile。
laiyuan：guangyuanxinwenwang●10yue23ri，lizhouqu（hanjingkaiqu）xinzeng20lixinguanfeiyanwuzhengzhuangganranzhe
●guangyuanshilizhouqujuxingxinguanfeiyanyiqingfangkonggongzuoxinwenfabuhui●qingzhuanfa！bieyinsheyixingweiliuxiaandi，yingxiangzijihejiaren！

# tadiaosijizhengzenmekao？baomingxuyaoshenmetiaojian？baokaozhuyishixiang！
lejiedenggengduobaokaoxinxi qingsaomiaoxiafangerweimatianjialaoshiweixinzixun 。
tadiaosijizhengzenmekao？baomingxuyaoshenmetiaojian？xianzaishehuishangdehenduogangweiduyaoqiuchizhengshanggang，qizhongjiubaokuotadiaosiji，shijianzhuxingyebukeshaodeyizhongzhiye，congshizhegezhiyexuyaoqudetadiaosijizheng，xiamianjiugendajiajieshaoxiazhengshudebaokaofangfahebaokaotiaojian。
tadiaosijizhengzenmekao
1、tijian
zaiqudetadiaogongzuozhengzhiqian，shouxianyaoxianganjianbumenshenqing。huodeshenqingzhihou，jinxingtijianquerenshifoujuyoutadiaodexiangguanshentitiaojian。tijiandifangkeyixuanzedangdidejibingkongzhizhongxinhuozhezhengguiyiyuan，zuowantijianhou，qudekaoshirenyuandejiankangzheng。
2、zhunbeixiangguancailiao
anzhaoxiangguanguiding，congshitadiaoxingyedenianlingguidingwei18sui~60suizhijian。nianlinghegedehua，zhunbeishenfenzheng、xuelizhengming（biyezheng）jiukeyile。kaoqutadiaozhengduixuelideyaoqiubingbushihengao，yongyouchuzhongyishangwenhua，zaishenheshijiukeyiguoguan。
3、tianxieshenqing
nazhaokaoshirenyuandexiangguancailiao，laidaodangdidexingzhengfuwuzhongxindating。zhaodaoanjianjudechuangkou，jinxingbaoming。daodifanghou，gongzuorenyuanhuigeiniyizhang《tezhongzuoyeanquanjishupeixunkaoheshenqingbiao》，genjushenqingbiaoshangdeyaoqiu，jinxingtianxiejiukeyile。
4、baomingchenggonghou，jinxingpeixun
peixundedifangyibandushizaishehuipeixunjigoujinxingde。wulunnagedifang，duhuiyouzheyangyigejigoucunzai。ruguobuzhidaodifang，keyishangwangchaxunyixia。huozhezaitianxieshenqingbiaodeshihouxunwenyixiagongzuorenyuanyekeyi。zheyangdepeixundushixuyaojiaonayidingfeiyong，butongdifangfeiyongbutong。
5、peixunwanchenghou，jiukeyikaishikaoshile
peixunshijianyibanwei15tianzuoyou。dangpeixunwanchenghou，jiukeyidaozhidingdifangjinxingkaoshile。kaoshijieshuhou，dengdaikaoshijieguofabu。
6、lingqutadiaosijizheng
zaikaoshihegehou，daizhaoxiangguanzhengshuhefuyinjianyijizijideyicunmianguanzhao，zaidaozhengwufuwuzhongxinanjianchuangkoulingquzijidetadiaosijizheng。
**baokaotiaojianruxia：**
genju《tezhongzuoyerenyuananquanjishupeixunkaoheguanlibanfa》
disitiao benbanfasuochengtezhongzuoyerenyuanshizhizhijiecongshitezhongzuoyederenyuan。
1、nianlingman18zhousui；
2、shentijiankang，wufangaicongshixiangyinggongzhongzuoyedejibingheshengliquexian；
3、chuzhongyishangwenhuachengdu，jubeixiangyinggongzhongdeanquanjishuzhishi，canjiaguojiaguidingdeanquanjishulilunheshijicaozuokaohebingchengjihege；
4、fuhexiangyinggongzhongzuoyetedianxuyaodeqitatiaojian。
**tadiaosijishizuoshenmede**
tadiaosijishifuzeyunxingjianzhugongdishangzuichangyongdeyizhongqizhongshebei，yiyijieyijiedejiechang（gao），haoxiangyigetietadetashiqizhongjiderenyuan，zhuyaogongzuoshidiaoshigongyongdegangjin、muleng、jiaoshouguandengshigongyuancailiaodeshebei。shijianzhushigongyizhongbibukeshaodeshebeiyunxingrenyuan

# kesouruhediaolini？quancishidaigongnengshoushi！（dongguanquancigongnengshoushizhaoshangjiameng，shenzhenquancigongnengshoushizhaoshangjiameng）
geijiarensongshenmeliwuhaoni？zuijintianqihulenghureyouhenduopengyouduranshanglekesou，xiamianxiaobiangeininjieshaoyikuanqianwugurenhouwulaizhedeyizhong“ci”shoushi，bujinwaiguanhaokan，guanjianhuannengyufangjibing~gaishanxieyexunhuan~shisonggeizijijiarenhuozhepengyoudebuerliwuzhixuan。
tianqizhuanliang，henduorenhuanshanglekesou。yanjiubiaoming，kesoushirentideyizhongzhongyaofangyujizhi。danghuxidaoshoudaoguoduofenmiwuhuoyiwudecijishi，jiuhuichanshengqingweidekesou，jiangqipaichutiwai。jinguankesoushiyizhongbaohuxingfanshe，danpinfandikesoukenengjiumeinamejiandanle！manxingkesoukanchixushijian！3zhouyineidejixingkesouduoyuganmao、zhiqiguanyan、feiyandengyanzhengyouguan。
zhexiebinghaole，kesouyehuisuizhixiaoshi。danyaozhuyi，ruotanliangjiaoduo、weinongxinghuangsetan，kenengshixijunganran，yaolingwaijiuzheno！3~8zhouzhijiandeyajixingkesou，daduoshihuxidaoganranhouyiliudezhuangtai。zhezhongqingkuangyaoxiangzhizhukesou，yaojishijiuyi。chaoguo8zhoujiukenengshimanxingkesou，bixujiuyi，paishexiongpian，quanmianjiancha！
**manxingkesoudebingyin**
manxingkesouzhuyaoyouwudabingyin：kesoubianyixingxiaochuan（CVA）、shangqidaokesouzonghezheng（UACS）youchengbihoudiliuzonghezheng（PNDS）、shisuanlixibaoxingzhiqiguanyan（EB）、bianyingxingkesou（AC）、weishiguanfanliuxingkesou（GERC）。danzuichangjiandehuanshiqianliangzhe！
**kesoubianyixingxiaochuan**
zheqishishiyizhongguomin，youyan、lengkongqi、huichenduhuiyinfakesoubianyixingxiaochuan。zhuyaozhengzhuangyuxiaochuanxiangsi，biaoxianweijulieganke，yejiankesoubibaitianzhong。huanzheyaojishijiuzhen，yidanquezhenyaojinzaozhiliao，liyongyaowuzhiliao，tongshitigaoziwojibingguanliyishi，jinxingchangqichixuzhiliao。
**shangqidaokesouzonghezheng**
yinfashanghuxidaokesouzonghezhengdechangjianbingyinyouputongganmao、biyan（tebieshiguominxingbiyan）、bidouyan、bixirou。huanzhezaibingqingfazuoshipinfanqinghou，yanyangbushihuobiyangbisai、liutipenti，shenzhishengyinsiya。zaizhiliaoshi，yizhiliaobiziweizhu，jianshaofenmiwudaoliu，yikongzhikesou。manxingkesouzenmeban？faxiankesouchangqimeiyouhaozhuanzhihou，yidingyaoquyiyuanjiuzhen，quanmianjiancha，quedingbingyin。zunxunyizhu，peihezhiliao。tongshizaishenghuozhongjinliangbimianjiechuguominyuancijihuxidao，daozhibingqingjiazhong。danshiwomenduzhidao，xiaochuanhennangenzhi，zhinengtongguoyaowushouduanjinxingkongzhi，jinliangjiangdifabingpinlv。kuangqiezaishenghuozhong，wufawanquanbimianyuwaijiecijiwujiechu，suishiduyoukenengjiazhongbingqing，shenzhiweijishengming。zheshiciliaodezuoyongjiutixianchulaile！
**nameciliaoyoushenmezuoyongni？**
ciliaonenggougaishanfeibudexieyexunhuan，gaishanzuzhitongtouxing，youliyuyanzhengdexiaosanxishou，tigaomianyili，rangbaixibaogengjiahuoyue，tunshilizengqiang。yifangmiannenggouyuyaowuzhiliaojinmipeihe，zengqiangliaoxiao；lingyifangmiankeyiconggenbenshangzengqiangrendetizhi，zengqiangjitimianyili，gengjiajiankang。
jingchangkesoukebiedayile！guanzhushentijiankangcongxiaoshikaishi！ciliao，shouhuojiankang，shouhuoxingfu！

# dianshibeijingqiangzhuangxiude5dawuqu，henduorenbeikengle，dubuzhidaowentichuzailena？
ketingzhuangxiudezhongxin，bushaorendufangzailedianshibeijingqiangshang，cuodaoshimeiyoucuo，danyibuxiaoxinjiuyongliguomeng，cailexiamianzhe5gewuqu，xiwangdajiayinyiweijie，buyaolvlvrukeng！
wuquyi：beijingqiangdashounaguiyueduoyuehao
gangxuhuxingdaduogengzhongshishenghuo，youqishiyoulehaizihou，henbudezijiazhuangxiuquanwudamanguizi，jinyimiandianshibeijingqiangdudamanle，qishiyoushihouwanquanmeibiyao，bijingrujindingzhiguiyebubianyi。
dianshiqiangdaodiyaobuyaozuoshouna，zanmenshigenjuquanwuchuwuliangjuedingde，qianwanbuyaoxiangdangran，burannazhendeshilangfeiqian。
wuquer：beijingqiangdengguangyueduoyueliangyuehao
dianshibeijingqiangzaiketing，henduorenduyongdengdai、shedeng、bidengdengzuozhuangshi，henbudemeigejiaoluoduyongshang，kanzhaodequeshiliangtangle，danquemeiyounixiangyaodedangcigan，yidanduolehenrongyizaluan，youqibutongdedengjuyoubutongdeshiyongxiaoguo，zanmenyaozhuoqingkaolv。
wuqusan：beijingqiangcailiaoyueguiyuehao
buzhidaoshenmeshihou，dajiaxingchengleyizhongrenzhi：guidecailiaojiushangdangcijiuhaokan，qishibingbushizheyang，dianshibeijingqiangzenmezhuang，shigenjuzhengtijiazhuangjuedingde，bingbushiyueguiyuehao，zhiyaoyongduile，napajinshirujiaoqiyenenghenchucai。
